READ ME: This is a plain-text convenience edition for reading, selecting,
copying, commenting and making edits in Notepad. You do NOT need a ZIP
extractor or an HTML editor for this file.

SOURCE STATUS: These 7,777 are PROPOSALS, not ratified Virelanthar canon.
677 proposed headwords + 7,100 composed sample phrases.
The original 346 normative words from the GitHub source are NOT included
in this 7,777 count; their canonical forms remain unchanged.
Source: https://github.com/BEKW-code/Virelanthar
Each numbered line: [ID] VIRELANTHAR || ENGLISH || FRANÇAIS
Double pipes delimit fields. Preserve punctuation and accents.
For actual machine import, use the original .csv/.json files.

NOTEPAD SAVING:
1. Use File > Save As > Encoding: UTF-8; keep the original extension.
2. For this reader copy, .txt is intentional. For HTML, save .html; for
   JSON, save .json; for CSV, save .csv. Choose Save as type: All files.
3. Do not open the ZIP as text or assume renaming .txt to .html converts it.
4. Keep the original exported machine-readable file as backup.

VL-P-06971 | Mi ne so shavir ei navor. || I will not restore the machine. || Je ne vais pas restaurer la machine.
VL-P-06972 | Mina ne so shavir ei navor. || We will not restore the machine. || Nous n'allons pas restaurer la machine.
VL-P-06973 | Ti so shavir ei navor ke? || Will you restore the machine? || Vas-tu restaurer la machine ?
VL-P-06974 | Tira so shavir ei navor ke? || Will you all restore the machine? || Allez-vous restaurer la machine ?
VL-P-06975 | E shavir ei navor. || Restore the machine! || Restaure la machine !
VL-P-06976 | Sha e shavir ei navor. || Please restore the machine. || Veuillez restaurer la machine.
VL-P-06977 | Mi so shavir ei navor ra Ralim’e hi. || I will restore the machine, in a spirit of harmonization. || Je vais restaurer la machine, dans un esprit d’harmonisation.
VL-P-06978 | Mina so shavir ei navor ra Voh’resh he. || We will restore the machine, in a spirit of transformation. || Nous allons restaurer la machine, dans un esprit de transformation.
VL-P-06979 | Mina so shavir ei navor ra El’shava ha. || We will restore the machine, remembering wholeness. || Nous allons restaurer la machine, en souvenir de la complétude.
VL-P-06980 | Mina so shavir ei navor ra Tulan’var ha. || We will restore the machine, in a spirit of radiant will. || Nous allons restaurer la machine, dans un esprit de volonté rayonnante.
